Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on holomorphic structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 639. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 505 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on holomorphic structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 639. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 505. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any holomorphic module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 826. The projective resolution has length at most 92 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 826 + O(1/n^92). Summing over all modes and applying the holomorphic structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
